- [x] **🗓️ Last season's tournament outlived the roll-forward (prod, Milton Bats U13 Purple, 2026-09-12 — FIXED on dev the same day, no migration; owner ruling the same day: the Tournaments page and the Overview tile are THIS SEASON'S too)** — the Schedule's read-only tournament-game feed and the Tournaments list both read the team's lifetime registration record with no season boundary; after a roll the calendar correctly held none of last July's games, so nothing hid the chips, and the list and tile went on counting a tournament the new season never played. - [x] **📒 Budget dates, and the "to date" reading — owner-ruled and mockup-approved 2026-09-04 (proposal `9bc53080`; gate `91368ba1` — the plan list, the statement's control row and the amended line form, all at true size); committed `394ae7b0` 2026-09-05, no migration; Owner QA §145 ✅ PASSED 2026-09-05, 42/42, zero defects — COMPLETE, plan pair archived** — every budget line answers **"when does this money move?"** in MONTHS with **"No date yet"** as a real third answer, and the report gains a second basis, **To date**, that the answer makes possible. The line form asks it after Amount, **nothing pre-selected and no month pre-filled** (a pre-picked month makes a plausible-looking date the fastest way out of the form, which is the day-grain failure one level up), Add Line off until answered, no explanatory sub-lines, the month picked with the split editor's own year-grouped dropdown (24 options — never a chip row), and one consequence line under the exception saying what it costs; the optional **Split by period** checkbox is DELETED, since splitting becomes one of the three answers. The plan list's existing **Schedule** column is REWRITTEN as **When** — it named chunk counts and read `Mar · 2 chunks` over a line whose $1,000 balance had no date — with a `When` filter, a one-tap "N lines have no date" bar, and the answer moving under the line's name at ≤640 where the column is `display:none` today. Budget vs Actual gains **Compare** (Whole season default · To date) on the Statement AND By activity, never on Months; under To date the plan column counts only plan money dated on or before today on both sides, its heading becomes **Plan to date**, budgeted dues become instalments due on or before today, and the closing row is renamed **Net to date** (under that basis it is a cash-timing figure wearing a profitability name — a team whose dues start in October reads deeply under water while its bank balance is fine). A3's apology sentence becomes a door under Whole season and is DELETED under To date; its neighbour keeps its figure and changes one verb. No new column — a line's answer IS its periods, and nothing reads "answered no" vs "never asked". ⚠ Excluded money has two causes and only undated money is a gap; money dated after today is the basis working. **§145's four open judgment calls were all settled in the walk** (no separate "Net to date" sentence, fix-it bar stays line-count-only, default stays Whole season, and per-chunk dates stay advisory even in Specific dates) — see the ledger's Verdict section. (see docs/projects/archive/COACH_BUDGET_DATES_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md + _BUILD_PROMPT.md)

- [x] **🔐 Supabase stops auto-granting new tables on 2026-10-30 — every new table now grants the server its own access, build-enforced (2026-09-24 · no migration · no customer-visible change)** — Supabase's platform change leaves any table created after the date unreachable through the Data API until it is granted explicitly, and its revoke also cancels the server-side default this repo set for itself in May, so the next new table would have failed for the server's own admin client — on prod only, when a migration reaches dev before the date and prod after, invisible to the drift gate. Existing tables are untouched. New rule for every migration after 309: grant the server select/insert/update/delete on each table it creates; grant signed-in or anonymous users only when the browser genuinely reads it (unchanged posture — dev already withholds those). Held by a new unit guard (self-tested, including a check that it can see every live table a migration created); written into the `/db` and `/dba` checklists. Prod's live default permissions were not read (not authorized) — confirm with a read-only query when convenient. See docs/agents/db/DB_ARCHITECTURE_REVIEW.md Finding #43.
- [x] **✍️ "Prepared … · a coach" — the portal learns everyone's name, and the handout stops needing one (owner report 2026-09-22)** — the development handout printed a byline naming the coach, and for most coaches that byline read **"a coach"**. Cause: every attribution line in the coach portal resolved names from the org membership's display name ALONE, and that column is written on exactly two paths (accepting an invitation; an admin typing one on the members screen) — so anyone who **signed up and created their own org or team** has never had one, and the result was backwards: an invited assistant was named, the head coach who owns the team was not. Signup has stored their real name on the account since it was written; nothing asked it. **Two fixes, two different decisions.** (1) **The handout names nobody at all** — owner ruling: not the blank word, and not the name either; it is the record of a player, not a signed document, and the coach handing it over is standing right there. The line is now just *Prepared <date>*, on the screen preview and the PDF alike (one model feeds both). (2) **Everywhere a name genuinely matters** — notes, observations, the player development section, the session record grid, the closed-season history panel — the lookup now falls back to the person's own account name, so all five correct at once. Ordering is deliberate: the club's word for someone wins (an admin may have set it, and the same account can be "Rob" in one club and "Coach Cowan" in another), the account name is the fallback and never the override. **⚠ And the fallback is GATED on still being a member of that club** (adversarial review caught the first cut): taking a coach off a team deletes their club membership row once they hold no other seat, which is precisely what used to make a departed coach read "a coach" — an ungated account lookup has no org filter and would have put the real name of somebody the club REMOVED back onto every record they ever wrote. The gate costs the fix nothing, which is what makes it the right shape rather than a compromise: the head coach this exists for **has a row, it is merely blank** (dev: 20 of 21 active head coaches, all 36 assistants). "Has a row, no name" is the bug; "has no row" is a person the org let go, and "a coach" is the honest answer for them. The account tier is also **self-asserted** — the holder can rewrite it from the browser, unlike the club's word — so it is stripped of control/bidi characters and capped at the members screen's own 60, and the best-effort lookup carries a **1.5s deadline** (a `catch` covers auth being down; it does nothing for auth being slow, and these reads were pure-Postgres before). Bonus from the same root: the practice plan's staff picker fell back to the **email's local part** whenever the member name was blank, so "People on this team" had been offering people as `b2cowan`; it now offers real names. Also retired a hand-rolled second copy of this same name ladder that one screen already carried. Verified: `npx next typegen` + tsc clean, unit **4519/4519**, `verify:changed` exit 0, and a probe against the dev database proving all four rungs (blank row → account name; club-named row → the club's word still wins; unknown user → stays unnamed so "a coach" still fires; and the account metadata shape itself). No migration. Help article corrected in the same unit of work — it documented the old byline verbatim.

- [x] **🧠 Coach development lifecycle — re-evaluated end to end, CLOSED 2026-09-16** — Phases 0–3 (record integrity, the Development grant, recording both test results and observed skills, and the first Reports & handout) were followed by a nine-station holistic walkthrough that re-drew the whole area stage by stage, ruled one stage at a time: **Arrive** (a real getting-started-then-dashboard Overview, no door at all without the Development grant), **Define** (one word per thing — test/skill, result/observation/attempt), **Session** (attempts are planned on the session, not the metric), **Player** (a goal opens in place, Results is a table, a status change is a dated review), **Reports & handout** (Insights → Coverage is the one roster table, the chart's caption is a legend, a rebuilt four-line family handout and season recap). - [x] **🔑 The assistant-coach invite knows you already have an account (owner-reported 2026-09-10 · QA owed · no migration)** — the accept screen asked one question, *are you signed in right now?*, so a returning assistant who was merely signed OUT got a full "Set up your account" form for an email that already had an account, with "Sign in instead" as a footnote. That form could never succeed — submitting it 409s and bounces to sign-in — so the flow was correct and unusable. It now has **four** states: signed in as the invited coach (one-tap accept, unchanged) · signed in as **someone else** (named up front with a sign-out-and-continue button, instead of a confident green accept that could only 403 — the wrong-account email was already in the payload and the page ignored it) · signed out **with** an account ("Welcome back", sign-in as the primary button, password reset as the escape) · signed out with no account (today's form). The "does this email have an account?" check is answered off the **invite row**, never a query-string address, so it is not an enumeration oracle, and it fails **open** — a lookup blip degrades to the old form rather than breaking an accept. Three copies of that check across the platform (two of them a weak single-page scan that reports *false* for any account past row 1000) collapse into one paginating, fail-closed helper. Guarded by `tests/unit/assistant-invite-account-states.test.ts` — every broken state is invisible in the happy path, which is how the original shipped.

- [x] **🗂️ The category is the shelf — one grouping rule for money in (✅ QA §158 PASSED 69/69 walked on 2026-09-10, zero defects, nothing flagged · **all three asks RULED AS BUILT** · committed `8921d864` on dev 2026-09-09 · no migration)** — money in now reports under the category it was filed in on **every** surface: the plan list, its by-period grid, both plan files, Budget vs. Actual's Months view and the Statement. *Tournaments* is a peer heading rather than a shelf inside *Other income* (finishing the unbuilt half of the 2026-09-08 fundraising ruling, and superseding Months' 08-23 "by source" grouping for the three funding groups only); *Sponsorships* → *Sponsorship* and *Other income* → *Other Income*, one spelling each; the by-period grid's nameless cost bucket takes the one spelling too ("No category" — it read "Uncategorized" while its own nameless-last sort compared against the other word, so the rule had never fired). The Statement's unclaimed sponsor pool moves off the invented "Not in the plan" heading onto **Sponsorship (not budgeted)**, which is what put the report's two views back into agreement about one cheque. The 57-chip "What am I forgetting?" wall becomes an **index**: two direction headings in the form's own words, one button per category with its count, chips only inside the open category. ⚠ Found by the owner from screenshots — `check:money-report` proved every total agreed and had nothing to say about the headings, so the new **claim 5b** (the revenue categories on Months equal the Statement's) was written, and it failed in both directions on its first run. **The walk's three asks were ruled as built on 2026-09-10** — the Statement's shelf wording (E1), the chips sitting below the button row rather than attached to their button (F3), and the open category not surviving a reload (F5); reasons in QA ledger §158. `/simplify` (eight cleanups) + `/review` (high-risk tier — two stale help sentences, the Months grid's inert sticky heading, a compile-time guard on the income-source switch) run 2026-09-09. **Two follow-ons outlived the plan and are tracked as their own items:** the importer's direction gap (now "The plan file reads back whole", owner QA §165) and the standard vocabulary review (see docs/projects/archive/COACH_BUDGET_CATEGORY_IS_THE_SHELF_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md)

- [x] **💰 Coach fundraising — one way in (✅ QA §157 PASSED 74/74 walked on 2026-09-10, zero defects, nothing flagged · committed `1b06f9e7` 2026-09-08 · migration 285 on prod 2026-09-08, job 262)** — the category a budget word sits in decides who fills its number in and where it reports: Fundraising and Sponsorship words are always filled in from the Fundraising tab (each drive and sponsor names the line it is *raising for*, pre-filled, filtered to its shelf, the team's own words included) and are REMOVED from "Other money in"; "the whole team" becomes a drive entry with no player and no family share (load-bearing — the only way team-raised money gets in); a line reports under its category's shelf; Tournaments stays other income. Fixed five verified defects incl. a drive having NO budget line (two planned fundraising lines both go blank) and the money form claiming grants in two answers. ✅ **F4 RULED — a whole-team entry IS allowed on a drive that pays a family share, as built**: the dash in the Credit cell and the separately-counted team entries are enough on their own, and the two alternatives (refuse above 0%, ask at save time) are refused rather than deferred, because a team running one drive with both a bake table and per-player selling would have nowhere to put the table's takings. The walk proved the money behaves: a $400 whole-team entry moved the drive's Raised tile and left *Credited to families* untouched, editing it to $450 followed and deleting a second one unwound cleanly, no family's dues balance moved at any point, and on the report Total revenue and Season net were unchanged while the revenue rows redistributed onto named lines — the sponsor pool splitting into *Team sponsorship* and *Grant* and adding back up. ⚠ **H1 (the demo world) was NOT walked — owner-waived, not owed** ("I don't need to check the demo"); `check:demos` was re-run and reports both worlds presentable, with assertions pinning the hoodie drive, its line, its one whole-team entry and that it credits nobody, so what is genuinely unverified is only three screen reads (the $480 on the *Merchandise sales* row, dues unmoved, and the mid-season team's Bottle Drive and sponsor now on named rows). ⚠ The walk also SURFACED A NEW PROJECT rather than a defect — the Record door hides every player who has already handed in, which is the friction behind "a player hands in more than once" (still proposed, above). Plan + PM brief archived to docs/projects/archive/COACH_FUNDRAISING_ONE_WAY_IN_PLAN.md.

- [x] **🪜 Coach Budget Plan — the plan adds up on the page (✅ QA §156 PASSED 40 of 43 walked on 2026-09-09, zero defects, nothing flagged · owner-approved on mockup round 2, 2026-09-08 · committed `e1aa4b6e` 2026-09-08 after `/review` · no migration)** — the List and By-period grid split into Costs and Funding bands, each closing on a subtotal that wears the tile's exact name (Planned costs · Planned funding), and the close reads as a ladder: Costs less funding → Player installments → Short of covering the plan; a season estimate's gap becomes rows under the costs; both exports follow. - [x] **💳 Coach Money — what a credit is, and what happens when you hand it back (RULED + BUILT 2026-09-07 · committed `ce3fb8ab` then `480a008a`, the two ceiling fixes `9d8fa6ae` + `6ae7a7e7`, the caption’s move to a footnote `f4a291f5` · migration 281 on prod 2026-09-08 (job 262) · **✅ OWNER QA §153 PASSED 45/45, zero defects, nothing flagged, 2026-09-09**, walk artifact `6f869cb5`, run-order B12 CLOSED)** — ⚠⚠ A CONFIRMED REPORTING DEFECT, LIVE TODAY AND NOTHING TO DO WITH ANY FEATURE: when a family pays a team bill out of their own pocket, Budget vs. Actual counts the cost as season spending and counts the dues credit that settled it NOWHERE, so the season reads worse by every reimbursement — **$1,379.98 on the UAT fixture, tying to the cent against the credits issued** ($1,179.98 expense-level + $200.00 payment-level, the P4 fronted-bill case). A second error runs the OTHER way: money already handed back to families is still counted as revenue — ⚠ **$300.00, not the $600.00 first written here** (the other $300.00 came out of fundraiser credits, which the Statement had already netted off fundraising, so it is uncounted rather than double-counted). Two mistakes in opposite directions, which is why neither showed up. Owner rulings 2026-09-07: **R1** the budget never moves; **R2** a family-paid cost counts on both sides of Season spending (*"the same as a parent paid their dues and the team paid for the item"*); **R3** a fundraiser rebate moves from Fundraising to dues (without it Kai reads $270.83 short instead of $70.83); **R4** own money handed back comes off dues (*"their net contribution to the team"*); **R5** a payback SELECTS the whole debts it settles — no free-typed amount, one or many per cheque. ⚠ CASH IS CORRECT AND MUST NOT BE TOUCHED; no balance moves and no dues are recalculated — the balances are already right, which is why this is a REPORT defect. ⚠⚠ DO R5 NOW: **production has ZERO payouts**, so there is no history to backfill and no guessed link to write — today is the cheapest this change will ever be. ⚠ Build the payout→credit link WITH AN AMOUNT even though whole-credit-only ships, or a future partial becomes a migration. ⚠ ONE CASE CANNOT SELECT A CREDIT and the code already says so — a season-surplus share *"is not a credit"* — so scope R5 to the Pay out sheet and leave settlement its own door. ~~⚠ OPEN: a hand-typed credit is money the product cannot trace~~ **RULED + BUILT: R6** a contribution is a **PAYMENT, not a credit**, and **R7** a fundraiser credit may not be typed by hand — it comes from the drive; `other` reads **Adjustment** on every surface. THE GATE: every family variance equals their Player Dues balance to the cent, TWELVE of twelve — ✅ met, asserted on the pure derivation rather than by eye (⚠ an earlier version of that gate was VACUOUS, reading 12/12 while comparing nothing; it now fails loudly on zero rows). ⚠ **ONE FIX BUILT AFTER THE COMMIT (committed `9d8fa6ae`):** a payback recorded before migration 281 says nothing about what it settled, so the new tick-list offered a $300.00 debt when $100.00 was owed — fixed by spreading legacy payouts own-money-first (plan §6d). ⚠⚠ **AND IT REACHED ONLY ONE OF THE TWO DOORS — fixed 2026-09-09 (plan §6f):** the SAVE never called that rule, so every family carrying a pre-281 payback met a refusal they could not satisfy and their remaining credit could not be handed back through any door — all SIX dev families, and reachable on prod the moment a coach settles the season and then pays a family back, because settlement paybacks carry no links BY DESIGN. Two further breaks of the same invariant went with it: the legacy spread ran newest-first because the ORDER was the caller's, and a forgiven balance could absorb a payback the ceiling excludes it from. The invariant is now written down and gated — what the tick-list offers may never exceed what the ceiling allows. ⚠ The review that followed found TWO pre-existing defects in the REPORT side (a write-off absorbing a payback moves the dues actual; a part-linked credit drops payout money and the two balances part by $50.00) — both **FIXED the same day** (plan §6g), and it was the same fix as §6f arriving a step late: the report gave the assumption only credits with NO link at all, and now gives EVERY credit the room it has left. A write-off can no longer swallow a refund (a season's dues actual stopped depending on the DATE a write-off was granted — $200.00 vs $100.00 for identical money), and the settlement's money stops going missing (settle a season then pay the family the rest: balance was $900.00 against the dues screen's $1,000.00, now $1,000.00 both). ⚠ NO STALE DATA NEEDED — the settlement writes paybacks with no links BY DESIGN. Zero families on dev hold the shape, so no figure moved. Plan docs/projects/active/COACH_MONEY_CREDITS_AND_PAYBACKS_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md; decision mockup https://claude.ai/code/artifact/0f08e331-7dda-456a-9836-c608f8a72eae; walk https://claude.ai/code/artifact/6f869cb5-8d38-4c39-8c30-5120ed99371d ✅ **OWNER QA §153 PASSED 45/45 on 2026-09-09**, zero defects, no step flagged — Kai’s story reads correctly out loud, the three lines behind the dues figure add to it, five named sponsors add to $2,085.75, Logan is offered $100.00 and Casey $37.50 rather than $300.00 and $337.50, a debt paid twice is refused in words, and Cash / every budgeted figure / Season spending are unmoved. **All three Part F calls RULED: (1) the caption on Player dues — *“it’s already removed”*: another session had moved it out of the table cell and into the report’s footnote stack (`f4a291f5`), which also got the sentence into the exported FILE for the first time — a treasurer was emailing a board a dues figure that counts a bill a family paid with nothing beside it saying so — and the §146 ruling that a set row says nothing is whole again; (2) help + the coach demo’s money narration — DO IT NOW, the deferral is lifted (its own item above); (3) the payback race — ACCEPTED: the cash cannot go wrong, only WHICH debt was recorded as settled, so it does not earn a transaction across four writes — written on the write path itself so a later session neither re-reports it nor closes it unasked.** ⚠ *Adjustment* was cleared on step 11d; the Owner QA Ledger had wrongly recorded that word as one of the three Part F calls, corrected there.

Plan docs/projects/active/APP_WIDE_TABLE_CONSISTENCY_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md. Rendered 319 times over 74 surfaces (coach 44 screens + 3 report views, tournament admin 14, platform 17, public 5) at 390/1440 in both skins. What the numbers showed: 37 row heights, 16 body sizes and 10 heading sizes on desktop; **15px is the app's commonest body size and no stylesheet asks for it** (a global cell rule beats every table-level size — the coach ladder never reached a list cell; ten platform tables render 15 against a declared 12); the coach list hairline is paper-coloured in warm (invisible on white); the register's zebra names a token that does not exist and has never painted; one global hover rule tints every row in the app; platform headings are 8.8px in sixteen hand-copied recipes; the Months view's figure doors are 26px on a phone. ⚠ NOT rendered: club-side admin (Families, Members, Rep teams, Accounting, House League) — the UAT org is tournament-tier and redirects, and the club fixtures have no admin member (a one-row fixture grant closes the gap); public league pages. Standard derived from the coach money grid with its private type ladder as the one documented exception (K-02). Register: 14 KEEP · 20 FIX · 7 ASK. Enforcement: generalise the money type-scale guard into a table-recipe guard (ladder membership + undefined-token check), add a control-WIDTH rule and a type-ladder membership rule to check:layout, and sweep the report views the default URL never shows. Docs: docs/agents/design/TABLE_AND_LIST_STANDARD.md · TABLE_EXCEPTION_REGISTER.md · TABLE_INVENTORY_2026-09-06.md; mockups + decisions https://claude.ai/code/artifact/0aa319dd-a6eb-4fff-b09b-df8591475fe1; prompt docs/projects/active/APP_WIDE_TABLE_CONSISTENCY_REVIEW_PROMPT.md ⚠⚠ THE WALK'S ONE DEFECT WAS A REGRESSION FROM THIS PROJECT'S OWN COMMIT: the Insights report tables (Playing Time, Results, Awards, Which-lineup-wins) had their column headings still on the left while their figures had moved right — the pass changed the figures and left the headings behind, so columns that had at least been *consistently* left became inconsistent. **Half a fix reads worse than none; when a pass changes one half of a paired treatment, go looking for the twin.** Reading the columns rather than the class also found the figure treatment applied to six columns that are not figure columns — the Results and Awards **Date** columns (dates are left, and on Results it is the first column, so the date had been shunted into the game name) and four figure-plus-words columns whose trailing qualifiers change length per row. All six back to left; "On field"'s share bar now leads its figure so the heading points at the number rather than the bar. Three rules generalised into the standard §3.4 and exception F-09 CLOSED. ✅ **A-08 RULED 2026-09-07 as recommended** — the admin shell keeps its 38px control height inside a table (K-18); the register's ASK column is now empty.

- [x] **🗂️ Coach Money — a budget line asks one question, not two (✅ QA §152 PASSED 41/41 on 2026-09-09, zero defects, nothing flagged · committed `2273e0b9` 2026-09-07 + `6976ec8f` 2026-09-08 · migs 280 + 282 on prod 2026-09-08, job 262)** — adding a budget line asked *"This line is…"* (Expense · Expected fundraising · Expected sponsorship · Expected other income) **and then** *"Category & Item"*, and the two answers could contradict each other because the item picker filters by DIRECTION and never by the kind just chosen. ⚠⚠ THAT WAS A TRAP, NOT UNTIDINESS: the kind decides where the row’s ACTUAL comes from, so *Expected sponsorship + Tournaments → Concession revenue* gave a coach a budget line whose actual is sought in sponsor cheques AND which refuses a typed figure — a row they could never report against, with nothing on screen saying why. The form now asks **ONE** question — *Money the team spends* / *Money coming in* — and the ITEM carries who reports its actual (**mig 280**: `budget_items.actual_source`, NOT NULL default `typed`, CHECK `typed|fundraiser|sponsor`, plus a CHECK that a money-out word is always typed). Each money-in option in the picker wears a tag — *From a drive* · *From a sponsor* · *You record it* — so a coach sees before saving whether the row will fill itself in. **Grant became sponsor-sourced** (it arrives as a cheque, and the sponsorship option’s own help text has always called a grant a sponsorship) while KEEPING its Fundraising category — moving the category is deliberately its own question. ⚠⚠ THE STORED ENUM IS UNTOUCHED and the 2026-08-16 ruling that sponsorship stays a distinct kind *because sponsor records depend on it* is intact: the three sources survive, only WHERE THE SOURCE IS DECLARED moved. The kind is now DERIVED server-side from the resolved item — **neither write door accepts a `lineKind` any more**, so the pairing is unexpressible rather than merely refused, and a guard test holds that over both routes. ⚠ NO FIGURE MOVED, measured not assumed: on dev AND prod every money-in budget line (5 + 1) points at a **platform** word and already used the obvious pairing, so all 48 dev lines derive exactly the kind they store; there are no club- or coach-created money-in words anywhere. ⚠ ONE ACCEPTED NARROWING (owner, 2026-09-07): a word a coach invents is born *typed*, so they can no longer have the drive machinery report against their own invented income word and use the standard *Fundraising drive* instead — nothing in either database used that. ⚠ Owner rulings taken before building: the question is a DROPDOWN not the mockup’s two radio rows (the 2026-08-22 convention, since a line’s direction is correctable forever), the item rows carry the TAG ALONE without the mockup’s duplicate sub-line (the 2026-09-04 no-filler-captions ruling), and the source lives as a COLUMN on the word rather than a name-keyed lookup (it survives a rename, and mig 248 deliberately allows two words to share a name). ⚠⚠ `/simplify` FOUND TWO REAL THINGS AND A THIRD FELL OUT OF THE SAME READING: the shared item picker was being taught a MONEY VOCABULARY (a boolean plus the tags’ English and CSS keyed to money values) inside a control the Budget Plan, the Club tab and the Org Budget all render — against that component’s OWN written rule, which already records a near-miss on the identical mistake one prop earlier; it now takes a domain-blind row-tag hook. **Three seed scripts — including the one that builds the PUBLIC DEMO — could still write a line whose kind disagreed with its word**, and mig 246 had made exactly this point about `direction` and named the demo seed among the paths it updated in the same unit of work; all three now derive, and the owner-QA fixture hard-stops on a disagreement. And the budget IMPORTER’s guard against overwriting a money-in line named only TWO of the three money-in kinds — its own comment claimed “both kinds” while a third had existed since 2026-09-02 — so an other-income line was open to being overwritten with a cost row’s word and amount. ⚠⚠ `/review` (high-risk, five lenses) FOUND A REAL DEFECT ON A DOOR THE REDESIGN NEVER LOOKED AT: **What am I forgetting?** offers standard money-IN words (rightly), but its chips opened the form on the SPENDING side with that word already chosen — the self-contradicting screen this project exists to remove. ⚠ It was WORSE before this project, which is why it survived: the server then trusted the form, so tapping *+ Grant* and saving stored the line as a COST, ADDING its amount to what families are asked for. Also fixed from the review: the derivation now THROWS on an unknown source (it returned `undefined`, which `JSON.stringify` drops, so Postgres substituted the column default and the row stored as a cost — silently); the demo seed’s kind lookup stops instead of falling back to ‘cost’; the fixture guard no longer disarms itself when its query fails. ⚠⚠⚠ RELEASE ORDER: **migrations 274 AND 280 must ride the same promote as this code** — production’s budget-line check admits only three money kinds, the fourth arrived in 274, and that fourth is what an ordinary typed word now derives, so code without both hands a coach a raw database error on one of the commonest money actions there is (including an ordinary amount edit). Recorded on run-order D1. ⚠ TWO CONSEQUENCES RECORDED RATHER THAN CHANGED: a future reclassification of a platform word re-files every line already on it at the next save (the design — only a migration can trigger it), and a team budgeting BOTH Team sponsorship AND Grant now has two sponsor claims in two categories so their sponsor money reports unplaced — the strongest argument yet for moving Grant to the Sponsorship category, put to the owner as walk Part H1. Gates: typecheck · 3,172 unit tests (16 new) · verify:changed · lint · layout sweep clean on `coach-budget` · all three seeders re-run with ZERO kind/word disagreements. ✅ **OWNER QA §152 PASSED 2026-09-09 — 41/41, no defects, no steps flagged**, walk https://claude.ai/code/artifact/a31b0e6c-c822-43cd-a086-a8cbdd9f1677 (run-order B11, now closed). **H1 RULED: Grant reads right on the Sponsorship shelf** — the open call is closed, and the earlier Grant move shipped as mig 282. ⚠ The walk was TRIMMED before it was taken: §156 and §157 landed in between and removed BOTH the per-row source tags this project shipped (a tag about where the app files money is a fact about our filing system, not about the coach’s money — owner 2026-09-08) and Part E’s invent-a-word case (§157 made the shelf decide who fills a word in), so the tag paragraphs above are this project’s history rather than today’s screen. ✅ The release-order dependency is spent: 274, 280 and 282 all rode the 2026-09-08 promote (prod HEAD `2e7ef905`, job 262). ### Coach portal page-level actions — every page header agrees, and a build check keeps it that way (2026-08-26)
- [x] **🔘 Coach portal page-level actions — the sequel to the header ruling (APPROVED 2026-08-13; ALL FOUR PHASES BUILT on dev; owner QA PASSED §105 + §110 2026-08-26; ARCHIVED)** — the header ruling said WHERE the actions slot is; this says which buttons go in it, how they're drawn, and which survive a phone. Inventory of all 40 team-hub screens found 10 carrying header actions, Import in four places under four names and weights (Roster's has no header home at all — a real defect: bulk-add is unreachable once one player exists), Export on four screens and missing from nine comparable ones, and — inside the Money hub alone — the same create job drawn lime-filled on two tabs and white-outlined on two others. Twelve rules, four phases. Money's header gains constant `Import ▾ / Export ▾` dataset menus (the tournament admin's Data Tools pattern, brought across) while every create drops into its own tab's existing control row, so the tab bar goes back to pure navigation; a Data Tools *destination* was considered and declined (it moves the button away from the moment of need). ⚠ **Rule 11 removes imports and spreadsheet exports from phone headers and therefore retires the paste-a-block path built precisely because phones have no file picker — the mitigation is mandatory, not optional: every empty state keeps its import offer at 390px.** ⚠ The cap came down from three buttons to two after the Plan templates render was drawn and rejected on sight; two of its three buttons turned out to be the same verb (now one `New template ▾`) and the third a library control that belongs with the list. Binding mockup: `claude.ai/code/artifact/44162825-32ef-4744-90dc-7939ee635e9e`. **Phase 1 (Money hub) BUILT on dev 2026-08-13** — hub-wide `Import ▾ / Export ▾`, all seven tabs' creates down into their own toolbars, creates unified to the lime fill, the three missing exports (budget lines, expenses & payables, fundraisers) written, "Recent imports" backed by migration 231 (**dev only — prod apply rides the next release**), and the empty payables list given the import door rule 11 depends on. **Follow-on 2026-08-13 (owner-directed):** Player Dues lost its budget cross-link banner — it pushed the coach out to a page to do a job that screen can now do in place — and its "Set dues for all players" button re-points at the **shared** Generate Installments modal, so there is one bulk-dues door instead of two divergent ones (the retired form had no preview of what any player would owe). The modal now owns its own budget read, carries the start-with-a-budget nudge as its empty state, and a re-run **replaces** the schedule — except for any player who has already paid something, who is skipped and reported. ⚠ That exception is a real fix, not a nicety: the bulk save it replaces deleted every installment for a player, **paid ones with their accounting entries included**. ✅ `/docs` run (the guide described a manual bulk path that no longer exists; rewritten + a new "can I redo the dues?" FAQ). ✅ `/review` run (high-risk tier, 5 lenses) — **3 Criticals confirmed and fixed, all three introduced by this change**: (1) the rewrite discarded its own write result, so a failure after the delete left a player owing a total with no schedule while the coach was told it worked — the schedule row is now secured BEFORE anything is deleted, every write is checked, and failures come back **named**; (2) the client decided "do dues already exist?" from a flag that counts only budget-generated ones, so a roster with any hand-set schedule dead-ended on a 409 quoting a "replace" option no screen offered — **the server is now the authority** and its refusal carries a code the modal turns into a question with the coach's form intact; (3) the extracted modal lost the visible-tab gate on its unsaved-changes guard, re-opening the app-wide click-hijack the tab-bar review already paid for once — `tabActive` now travels with the modal and both callers pass it. Also fixed: a stale preview response could restore a table the form no longer matched, and confirm had no re-entrancy guard. ⚠ **Two known defects left standing by owner ruling:** the preview table is computed from the budget while the write uses the typed amounts (**pre-existing**, predates this change, deferred to the upcoming modal upgrade), and a replace still overwrites hand-built per-player schedules and relabels them budget-generated (accepted as-is). Owner QA rides `OWNER_QA_LEDGER.md`. **Phase 2 (Roster + Schedule) BUILT on dev 2026-08-23**, to **four house rules the owner took that day** (plan §0) after reading the Money hub's own phone header back to it: Import lives in the header and never on a phone; **Export lives in the toolbar above what it exports, pinned right, at every width — never in a page header**; on a phone words become symbols, header and toolbar alike, and the symbol changes when the ACTION does; the create sits in the header beside the "?" and goes first. ⚠ The export rule is a PLACEMENT rule, not a contents one, and that is the point — Schedule's export takes the whole season in every view, so the older "does its content vary?" test said header while this says toolbar. One place to look wins. ⚠ Phone exports are cut by **file type, not width**: a spreadsheet lands in a downloads folder nobody opens, but a roster PDF gets held up to a parent and an .ics syncs a season into the phone in a coach's hand — deleting them wholesale was considered and rejected. Both screens dropped the tournament-admin export control (a different component, pinned to 32px with !important — §5.1's known conflict, now resolved for the coach portal) onto a new shared **CoachExportButton**; **MoneyExportButton became a thin wrapper over it, so no Money call site moved**. **Roster's two real defects are fixed**: bulk-add now has a findable Import door (it existed only in the empty state and behind a door inside Add Player), and the header no longer vanishes in the depth-chart view. ⚠ **The rendered sweep caught what nothing else could**: the relocated export landed at **30px at 390** — the portal enforces its tap floor through the SURFACE a control sits on, and a list toolbar was neither a page header nor a Money panel toolbar, so no rule reached it. Net baseline movement: **18 phone tap-floor entries removed, 0 added**. **Phase 3 BUILT on dev 2026-08-25** — Plan templates and Drills each had two creates competing in the filter row; both now carry ONE create in the page header with the two ways inside it, collapsing to a bare + on a phone. **Phase 4a BUILT on dev 2026-08-25** — the guard, and the correction of two rule texts the help-"?" move had made false. ⚠⚠ **Its first finding was that `npm test` ran NOWHERE automatically** — not in `verify:changed`, not in the pre-commit hook, not in the Amplify build — so every guard of this family, including the season/history ones, has been describing itself as failing "the build" while failing nothing. `npm test` is now the FIRST step of `verify:changed` (first, not appended: schema parity sits mid-chain and is chronically red on dev by design). The guard pins the ENUMERATION — 38 call sites across 35 screens, which pass header actions, their phone flags, no-export-in-a-header, create-first, not-gated-on-a-view, the Overview status-door carve-out and the two legal header shapes — while `check:layout` keeps COUNT and GEOMETRY, and the guard names the six rules that stay human review. **Ten deliberate violations were injected one at a time and each was caught.** Also closed portal-wide: `CoachToolbarMenu` now implements the ARIA keyboard pattern its `role="menu"` promised (arrows, Home/End, Tab-closes-and-returns), reaching Money Import, Plan templates and Drills. **4b open** — Schedule's hand-rolled Add Event menu and the last two hand-written header buttons, all held on files other sessions are mid-edit in. ### One clock, one spelling — the product stops telling the time two ways (2026-08-26)
- [x] **🕐 "8:00 a.m." everywhere a customer reads a time (owner + `/marketing` ruled 2026-08-26; build-enforced)** — the product showed the time two ways to the same family: a tournament schedule printed "8:00 PM" while the practice run sheet and the family schedule printed "8:00 p.m.". ⚠ The cause was not two conventions that grew equally — **seven hand-rolled builders** each constructed their own clock string with an uppercase token, while every screen that formatted through the platform was already correct. So the fix collapsed all seven into the one shared helper, which removes the duplication that let them drift apart in the first place and fixes the spelling as a side effect. Nine customer-visible literals swept (paste-error hints, a help article, an admin card preview, a worked example, the pre-sales walkthrough); code comments deliberately left alone, since several describe the original timezone bug in its own words. ⚠ The ruling rests on **house language, not positioning** — the brand canon forbids claiming Canadian identity, so it stands on the same ground as `colour` and `cheque`. Now gated: the spelling check fails on an uppercase token after a clock time, and also catches a narrow no-break space, which a future platform version may emit and which would be a third spelling that looks identical. Proved by reinstating both regressions and the invisible-space variant and watching the gate go red, and by confirming it stays quiet on bare acronyms.

- [x] **🧮 Coach Budget Plan — totals, expected funding, period columns (✅ OWNER QA PASSED 2026-08-13)** — four owner findings on one screen. The competing *Total Planned Budget* / *Season Total* tiles became a **summary ladder** that shows its working (line items → the un-itemized part of an optional *Estimated total* → total planned → less expected funding → per player); a budget line can now be **expected funding** (fundraising, sponsorship, grant) so dues are generated off what players actually fund; the plan gained its own **month / quarter columns** (they existed only on Budget vs. Actual); and the duplicate foot link went. Owner rulings baked in: the un-itemized difference is **calculated, never typed**, a negative difference is **shown in red, never blocked or ignored**, per player follows **the estimate whenever one is set** — including below the lines — and expected funding is one estimated total with **no link to individual campaigns**, measured against the **team's share** of what was raised. Header reworked 2026-08-13 from an owner screenshot: the year moved into a **band spanning its months** (the payoff is a season crossing New Year), headings sized up, and coach-typed line names stopped being uppercased by the platform-wide `th` rule they had inherited by being marked as row headings for screen readers.
- [x] **⚠ MIGRATION 230 MUST REACH PROD BEFORE THIS CODE DOES** — it adds a budget line's kind (cost / expected funding). Applied to **dev 2026-08-12**. `check:migrations` and the schema-parity gate both fail until prod has it, which is the guard working — **do not re-baseline either.**
- [x] **A build-failing guard now owns the rule the migration comment could only ask for:** `tests/unit/budget-line-kind-guard.test.ts` allow-lists every reader of `rep_budget_lines` across `app/ lib/ components/ scripts/`, each exception carrying a written reason. It exists because `/simplify`'s altitude lens found **three existing readers that had never heard of the kind** — season rollover silently reclassified funding lines as costs on carry-forward (a write path: permanent and invisible), and both the installment preview and the tryout accept-drawer suggested dues off gross cost. Proven to fail with a planted kind-blind reader.
- [x] **Two defects only a browser could see**, both caught by probes reading computed styles: the over-plan number was **not actually red** (a compound selector outranked the danger class), and fixing the row-name styling silently stripped the category/funding rows — two cascade collisions in one stylesheet. Rendered layout sweep clean; **12 tap-floor baseline entries pruned**.
- [x] `/simplify` (4 lenses, 12 fixes) · `/review` (high-risk, 5 lenses — an export that contradicted the screen, two surfaces disagreeing on per-player, a wrong-cause error message, a stale-response race in the estimate editor) · `/docs` (four rewritten passages, two new FAQs, one existing FAQ that had quietly become wrong, search verified in-browser). The new cross-table fundraiser filter was **proven** against the live dev DB rather than argued.
- [x] Owner elected to commit this alongside the in-flight Money-hub work (2026-08-13). Plan + PM brief + binding mockup archived → `docs/projects/archive/COACH_BUDGET_TOTALS_FUNDING_PLAN.md`.
- [ ] **⚠ OPEN, needs an owner ruling — not part of this project:** `rep_budget_lines` read access is keyed on **org membership alone** (confirmed against live `pg_policies`, not the migration) — not team assignment, not the `money` capability. Pre-dates this work (migration 028), but this work put fundraising and sponsorship targets behind that same gap. Same shape as the `rep_roster_players` gap migration 212 closed.

### Coach budget line — period split rework (2026-08-12)
- [x] **🗓 Coach budget line — period split rework (✅ OWNER QA PASSED 2026-08-12)** — three owner-reported defects on the Add/Edit Budget Line modal, built to an approved 4-round mockup. (1) A save that couldn't go through printed its reason into a strip at the bottom of a *scrolling* body, so it was usually off-screen and the button read as broken; now a failed save **scrolls to the offending row, focuses it, marks it, and shows a counter in the sticky footer** that clicks back to the first problem — deliberately not a second dialog (the form already stacks a discard guard, and a dialog still can't say which of twelve rows is wrong). (2) **Period labels are no longer mandatory** — a period with a month/quarter/date names itself ("Apr 2027"), shown greyed in the field before it's saved and overwritable; the client resolves the name on save so the NOT-NULL column and the API contract are untouched. Dates are optional too (undated rows carry a visible advisory that Budget vs. Actual can't place them). Only money can block a save now. (3) **A line is split by months / quarters / specific dates / just names** — each row carries its own picker FIRST and the optional label second, "+ Add period" auto-advances the month so twelve taps + Split evenly builds a year, plus "Fill the season" and "Clear all periods". ⚠ **Changing the split mode RESETS the periods** (owner ruling — converting 12 months onto 4 quarters piled 3 rows per quarter and mapping back produced three Januaries; months→dates is lossless and deliberately NOT carved out). Bulk actions offer **Undo instead of a confirm dialog**. No migration, no API change, no new fetch — the month/quarter selection *is* the period's date, so BvA / month grid / installments are untouched; a saved line's mode is re-derived on open. New pure module + 28 unit tests (1636/1636 green); typecheck ✓ · verify:changed all green ✓. (see docs/projects/archive/COACH_BUDGET_PERIOD_SPLIT_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md)
- [x] Owner QA passed 2026-08-12. Plan + PM brief + binding mockup archived → `docs/projects/archive/COACH_BUDGET_PERIOD_SPLIT_PLAN.md`.

### Next 16.2.4 → 16.3.0 framework upgrade — the security line closes (2026-08-12)
- [x] **LIVE ON PROD 2026-08-12** — promote job **254 SUCCEED** (13:42 ET), prod HEAD `396bd7cc`; staged through dev job 251 with the sharp/native icon path verified on the **deployed** environment before promoting; V9 live smoke green (pages, both demo doors, both icon routes byte-identical to local, 0 ERROR / 0 detect-libc events); build 5m27s — slightly faster than 16.2.4's. **All 21 published advisories against 16.2.4 (11 High, incl. the proxy-bypass cluster) are closed on prod.** Soak waived by owner — no active prod customers; prod is the bake.
- [x] **Measured before/after with the committed instrument** (`npm run measure:memory`; archived plan §12 is the permanent record): per-COMPILE memory fixed for real — coach Overview 262→72–82 MB, the coach help hub went from a 3/3-deterministic dev-server kill to a 456 MB survivable compile, the full 15-route walk ends at 11% of the ceiling, and the layout sweep is finishable again (it caught 7 tap-floor findings belonging to the 2026-08-11 header rework → header Pass-2 thread). Per-REQUEST drip NOT fixed (2.06→2.00 MB/req, open vercel/next.js#85666) → supervisor + heap ceiling + sweep guards **KEPT**, their docs reworded to the measured reality (restart-after-sweep relaxed on 16.3+). Prod runtime leak-free on BOTH versions (0.00 / 0.04 MB/req, flat). Artifact shrank 4.6 MB.

### Dev server memory ceiling made real + sweep watchdogs (2026-08-11)
- [x] **The dev server's memory cap had never been in force** — `package.json` asked for 3 GB on the command line, but `next dev` reads that limit from the `NODE_OPTIONS` environment variable only, discards a command-line one, and substitutes **half of installed RAM** (11182 MB here). The flag was inert for as long as it had existed. It cost two crashes read as "heavy sweep" rather than "no ceiling": 2026-08-02 (26 of 112 layout screens unmeasured) and 2026-08-11 (10.31 GB working set, machine at 1.7 GB free, dev server killed mid-sweep and again on auto-restart). `npm run dev` now routes through a launcher that sets the ceiling where Next actually looks — **owner-set 6 GB**, `DEV_HEAP_MB` to override — and warns if the number ever stops being below Next's own default. Proven both directions against Next's own resolver. `amplify.yml` was always correct and is untouched.
- [x] **Both browser-driving sweeps got a memory watchdog** — the layout sweep and the mobile capture refuse to start without headroom, abort mid-run if free memory falls through the floor (`DEV_FREE_FLOOR_MB`, default 1536 MB), and report their low-water mark on a clean finish. An abort exits non-zero **before** anything is written: a sweep that stopped early has unmeasured screens, and a baseline written from one records the product as cleaner than it is. Same reasoning as the existing refuse-partial-run guard, which an abort would otherwise slip past unnoticed.
- [x] **The dev server now recovers by itself** — the leak below means it dies during ordinary portal testing, and `next dev` exits outright rather than reviving, so the site silently stopped answering until someone noticed and restarted by hand. The launcher now supervises it and relaunches on the out-of-memory abort, saying loudly which restart number this is so the frequency stays visible instead of becoming background noise. Guarded against boot-loops (3 failures inside 15s of starting = a startup problem, not the leak — it stops and says so) and it never blames the leak for a crash that wasn't one.
- [x] **Root cause identified — it is Next's own development runtime, not our code** — measured over the inspector with forced GCs: ~2.7 MB retained per request with no plateau, ~126 of ~144 MB of surviving bytes traced to Next's dev-only runtime, our compiled chunks ~1 MB, and no unbounded accumulators anywhere in our code. **Not a sweep-only problem: ~21 coach pages of ordinary clicking exhausts it.** (2026-08-12 re-measure on shipped 16.3.0: the per-COMPILE half proved fixed; the per-request drip did not — open vercel/next.js#85666 — so the supervisor stays. See the completed upgrade entry above.)
- [x] **Written down as a standing rule** (AGENTS.md, beside the restart rules) — the dev server never releases a compiled route, so any full sweep leaves it permanently large and is itself a restart trigger; and the inert-flag shape is called out by name so a future cleanup pass cannot quietly reintroduce it.

### Rain Delay / Day-of Operations (2026-07-07)
*(Two complementary features — built, owner-tested, reviewed (2× /review, 0 Critical/High), and documented on **dev**, unpushed. **(A) Broadened fan notifications:** a public top-bar notification bell lets anyone opt into tournament-wide alerts (score / schedule / message categories) without following a team, and a posted day-of alert now **pushes** to opted-in fans + coaches instead of being a silent banner (mig **177** `fan_push_categories`, dev-applied/**prod-pending**). **(B) Bulk "shift the day" tool:** from **Tools ▾ → Rain delay** on the Schedule page, an organizer picks a day (today or any upcoming day), optionally **filters by division and/or venue**, and moves/cancels those games in one atomic action — server-side bracket-order hard-block, wall-clock (America/Toronto) time math, atomic via mig **178** `bulk_reschedule_games` RPC — then a prefilled **announce step** pushes fans + staff/coaches (new `tournament_announcement` notify event). Gated to **Tournament Plus** (`bulk_reschedule` key; /strategy logged + /billing wired 2026-07-07; free orgs keep manual reschedule + the free pinned banner + coach email). Also **reorganized the Schedule admin page** (Auto→**Tools ▾** with Build+Adjust groups; stage-aware header primary — Add Game in Round Robin / Build-Edit Bracket in Playoffs; Clear-bracket moved into the editor; removed the duplicate Conflicts chip) and fixed the shared Export button's mobile height across ~21 pages. /docs synced (Tools-menu location + Plus gate + free-plan guardrail + filters). Plan + brief archived: [RAIN_DELAY_DAYOF_OPS_PLAN.md](docs/projects/archive/RAIN_DELAY_DAYOF_OPS_PLAN.md) + [_PM_BRIEF](docs/projects/archive/RAIN_DELAY_DAYOF_OPS_PM_BRIEF.md). **Remaining to ship:** apply migs **177 + 178 to prod** → /marketing changelog → promote; **not committed** (awaits owner OK).)*

### J4-001 fix — Next 16 sync-params migration (2026-06-12)
*(18 client pages still used the legacy sync `params` signature, so in Next 16 they read `undefined` off the params Promise, fetched `/teams/undefined/...`, and rendered dead for every user: the rep-teams admin season cluster (program-year overview/coaches/schedule/tryouts, team overview, history ×2) and the entire premium coach accounting suite (overview, budget, budget-vs-actual, dues, expenses, fundraisers ×2, allocations, payment requests, documents, history). Found as fix-now finding J4-001 in the journey audit ([JOURNEY_J4_CLUB_PRESIDENT.md](docs/projects/active/journeys/JOURNEY_J4_CLUB_PRESIDENT.md)). Fix = minimal-diff migration to the official pattern: `params: Promise<...>` + `const params = use(paramsPromise)` as the first body line; bodies untouched. Verified: repo-wide residual-signature sweep clean (pages, layouts, route handlers), `lint:focused` 0 errors, full `typecheck` clean, live browser probe now fetches real IDs, program-year page renders roster/coaches/events. Uncommitted on feat/free-tier-coaches; unblocks the J2 walk (audit Phase 4).)*

### Stale tournament-slot cap fix — migration 121 (2026-06-10)
*(Bug: a Tournament Plus org saw "Your plan allows 3 tournament slots" when moving a tournament to draft. Root cause = stale `organizations.tournament_limit = 3` left over from when Tournament Plus was capped at 3 ([migration 037](supabase/migrations/037_tournament_plan_limits.sql)); after the cap was raised to unlimited (9999) in `lib/plan-config.ts`, `getEffectiveTournamentLimit()` still honors a stored finite cap verbatim for unlimited-tier plans — the intended platform-admin override path — so the stale value kept enforcing. Fix = data migration `121_lift_stale_unlimited_tournament_caps.sql` raising stored caps to 9999 for tournament_plus/league/club where `< 9999`, **excluding `[UAT_PROTECTED]` fixtures** (uat-plus-org/uat-club-org deliberately at 10). Applied dev (`dev-test-org` 3→9999; UAT fixtures preserved) + prod (0 rows — already clean, idempotent backstop). Data-only change: no schema/dictionary/snapshot impact. No code change — honoring a stored finite cap on unlimited tiers is the intended override mechanism.)*

### Release guardrail — prod migration safety (2026-06-09)
*(Migrations are never auto-applied by the release process — Amplify only builds, the `/release` agent only pushes git — so a dev-applied/prod-missing migration would 500 in prod, e.g. the migration-040 incident. Two guardrails added, local tooling: (A) `scripts/apply-migration-api.mjs` now takes a `--dev`(default)/`--prod` target so applying to prod is a deliberate one-liner; (B) new `scripts/check-prod-migration-drift.mjs` / `npm run check:migrations` queries both live DBs and exits 1 when prod is behind dev — wired into the `/release` agent preflight + promote gate + a hard rule. Verified: the check correctly blocks today on `basic_coach_team_players` (mig 114, dev-only).)*

- [x] **🖩 Scorekeeper score entry — mobile repair (2026-08-08, ✅ **DONE — LIVE on prod (2026-08-10 release, job 251)**; owner QA passed twice; ✅ `/review` run — 6 findings, all confirmed, 5 fixed; ✅ owner ruling: **three ways out became one** — the backdrop no longer dismisses (it was the only exit that could bin a half-typed score by accident, and with the keypad up it is a ~30px sliver) and the ✕ is retired, so **Cancel is the only way out, with Escape as its keyboard twin**; ⚠ **committed inside `3aeda5fd`, a concurrent agent's docs commit** — its own commit never ran, so `git log` will not lead anyone to this change; the plan doc is the record)** — the volunteer's score box computed to **~29px wide on every phone** (the −/+ steppers took 109 of the 138px each team gets), so the digit being typed could not be shown; the away score looked absent because it was. Steppers removed on an owner ruling — the sheet saves once and its own copy says the score is final immediately, so there was never a running tally to step. Also: the number pad now actually appears (the old input type let iOS and installed-PWA keyboards raise the full alphabet, and let a laptop scroll wheel change a final score), tapping a score selects it (correcting 5→3 gave 53), the keyboard's action key can no longer finalize, the header names the **game** (time · field · division) instead of repeating the team names printed below it, and the sheet now measures against the space the keypad leaves — Finalize previously cleared it by 5px. Mockups approved (artifact `f4bedded-…`, three options + a live keypad test). ⚠ **`check:layout` cannot see this screen** (no scorekeeper route in the sweep — it is behind a volunteer login); width claims are arithmetic, not rendered measurement, and closing that gap is the standing follow-up. Two UAT specs updated in the same change. (see docs/projects/archive/SCOREKEEPER_SCORE_ENTRY_MOBILE_PLAN.md + _PM_BRIEF.md)

- [x] **Date Correctness — "what day is it?" in the org timezone** — ✅ **COMPLETE 2026-07-26.** Production runs UTC while orgs schedule in Toronto, so every calendar-day answer ("is it game day?", "is this overdue?", "days until") was **a day late from ~8 PM Toronto every evening** — and invisible on a dev box, which runs Toronto time. Symptoms: tournaments flipping to *finished* mid-event on closing night, dues/payables flagging overdue 4–5h early, out-of-province coaches seeing a different date than the organiser. **76 sites / 59 files fixed**, four duplicate overdue checks collapsed into one, and a **guardrail at a ZERO baseline** (pre-commit + deploy) so it cannot return. Also unlocked unit-testing of non-leaf modules and added the missing `npm test` script (391/391 pass). Drift originates from the 9 tables hand-built per-environment BEFORE migrations existed (Apr–May 2026); nothing migration-created has drifted since. **✅ COMPLETE 2026-07-27 — 51 → 0.** Stages 2–4 (migs 201/202/203) applied to **dev AND prod**; `check:parity` baseline is now an **empty object**, so any future divergence fails the build immediately. P0 quick win: every notification type now has a distinct icon (added the 8 missing ones) + fixed the phone "square" push icon — the status-bar badge was a fully-opaque tile that Android alpha-flattens to a white square; now a transparent centered white chevron silhouette (84.5% transparent, verified), plus a borderless corner-clean Android maskable icon. `/design` approved + logged the direction (own transparent badge source; branded per-notification icon flagged as a follow-up). Service-worker cache bumped so installed devices refresh. Focused lint clean. The bell is one time-sorted list that becomes clutter on a busy tournament day (repeated "New registration" rows bury the one item needing a decision); the phone push icon renders as a square. Owner picked the **Full Act / Know / Talk** scope: pin **needs-attention** items (failed payment, coach access request, disputed score) on top, turn the rest into a **bundled, date-grouped activity feed** ("6 new registrations" not six rows), **move chat off the bell** onto the Chat tab's own unread badge (reuses the existing `useChatUnread`/`ChatUnreadBadge` already on the coaches shell), add a **"See all"** overflow page, and **fix the phone icons** (the status-bar badge is a filled square → Android paints it solid white; needs a transparent chevron silhouette). **No migration** — category + bundling are code-derived; delivery engine (notify/prefs/mute) untouched. 5 shippable phases (P0 icons+icon-map quick win → P1 categorize → P2 bundle → P3 chat-off-bell → P4 See-all). also consolidated to **one context-aware PDF** (bracket vs table by `layout` toggle) + removed iCal from admin export (kept public fan "Add to Calendar"). **Tier 3 #1 = Full Placement bracket BUILT 2026-06-05**: new `'placement'` format (`buildPlacement`/`rankInto` — every team ranked 1..N via championship + classification cascade; `PL{place}` deciding games; tested via terminal-ref→unique-1..N proof) **+ placement final-standings panel** (`placementPlaces`/`computePlacementStandings` → ranked 1..N on public Playoffs view). 45/45 bracket tests. **End-to-end usable.** **Tier 3 #2 (bezier double-elim tree connectors) + #3 (draw-day reveal animation) BUILT + browser-verified 2026-06-07** (50/50 tests; reveal restrained by design, owner OK'd; demo seed scripts/seed-bl-u15-doubleelim.mts on branded-light U15). **Split-pool (no-crossover) all-format brackets BUILT 2026-06-07** (each pool now runs single/consolation/double/placement via the unified engine; also fixed a latent `advancePlayoffs` cross-pool advancement bug by scoping to `bracketId`; awaiting browser verification). Remaining Phase 6: Tier 4 seed-by-number/prior-results seeding sources, pot draws, blank-bracket PDF. (Auto-scheduler double-elim dependency ordering VERIFIED correct 2026-06-07 — already builds the Winner/Loser dependency graph + generator hard-blocks out-of-order placement + unit-tested; no work needed.) Original scope: **one** bracket engine for ALL tournaments — any team count (with byes), single elimination / 2-game guarantee (consolation) / double elimination — same builder UI + look/feel everywhere. The **only** difference between tournament types is the seeding source: full tournaments seed from round-robin standings; new **bracket-only** tournaments skip round robin and let the organizer seed teams manually or by Randomize (resolved at creation). Key build: new pure `lib/playoff-bracket.ts` (seeding-agnostic generator: bye math + single/consolation/double-elim) replacing the hardcoded 2/4/8 templates in `PlayoffWizard.generatePreview()`; extend `PlayoffConfig.format`; double-elim/consolation reuse existing `advancePlayoffs` Winner/Loser routing (only new rule = the "if-necessary" grand final); tournament `format` flag in settings JSONB (no migration); Seed Teams wizard step for playoff-only; hide round-robin views on public + admin. Plus-gated. **Decisions locked 2026-06-04:** GF "if-necessary" reset = toggle default ON; V1 ships all three formats (single/consolation/double); placement = champion + optional 3rd place (full 5th/7th → Phase 6); Tournament Plus only (see [plan](docs/projects/archive/PLAYOFF_ONLY_TOURNAMENT_PLAN.md) and [PM brief](docs/projects/archive/PLAYOFF_ONLY_TOURNAMENT_PM_BRIEF.md))
